supply of services	[fair]	[fair]
skinvision provides the following online services:	[fair]	[fair]
the android or iphone smartphone application (app);	[fair]	[use]
the health insurance portal; and	[fair]	[fair]
the website www.skinvision.com (website).	[fair]	[fair]
in order to use the skinvision services, as described below, you need to login in the app by providing your email address as a username and creating a password.	[fair]	[fair]
you will be asked to confirm your email address within SPECIALNUMBER hours in order to receive a skinvision account.	[fair]	[fair]
you can personalize your account by choosing a profile picture and by completing the questionnaire of the risk profile and skin type.	[fair]	[fair]
depending on the country you are residing in, and what kind of service you have selected, the skinvision app provides the following services: skinvision smart check, skin check, health program and other services.	[fair]	[fair]
the goal of the skinvision online services is to help you monitor and track your skin over time, inform you about the health of skin, provide you with the opportunity to assess the risk profile of your skin and monitor the uv index of your location.	[fair]	[fair]
we aim to create higher awareness of your skin health by sending you information and reminders to keep you on track with your health journey.	[fair]	[fair]
skinvision smart check	[fair]	[fair]
the skinvision smart check is intended to provide an immediate risk indication for the most common types of skin cancer of a specific spot on the skin, based on a photo taken with your mobile device.	[fair]	[fair]
based on the assessment, the skinvision service provides a recommendation whether to visit a healthcare professional for further review.	[fair]	[fair]
the service creates skin health awareness and allows you to monitor spots on the skin over time and provides guidance in discussions with healthcare professionals.	[fair]	[fair]
skinvision performs continuous quality control of the smart checks with a team of algorithm and image recognition experts, collaborating with dermatologists.	[fair]	[fair]
in cases with a potential health risk, skinvision provides feedback to you about the preferred next step to take.	[fair]	[fair]
the goal of the smart check is to help you understand your skin health and is not intended to replace traditional methods of the evaluation of skin cancer risk level, is not a diagnosis, and is not a substitute for visits to a healthcare professional.	[fair]	[fair]
the skinvision service is not intended for use on children.	[fair]	[fair]
skinvision skin check	[fair]	[fair]
depending on where you are located, skinvision offers the skin check service.	[fair]	[fair]
skin check allows you to take a manual picture and directly submit it for review by a doctor.	[fair]	[fair]
our dermatologists will provide you with a risk indication and advice on the next steps to take.	[fair]	[fair]
the goal of the skin check is to help you understand your skin health when you are not able to take an automated picture and is not intended to replace traditional methods of the evaluation of skin cancer risk level, is not a diagnosis, and is not a substitute for visits to a healthcare professional.	[fair]	[fair]
the skinvision service is not intended for use on children.	[fair]	[fair]
skinvision skinhealth program	[fair]	[fair]
skinvision also provides a plan, which gives you access to unlimited smart checks while the plan is active.	[fair]	[fair]
at the end of the plan, you will be contacted that your plan is ending.	[fair]	[fair]
if you do not react or renew your plan, your account will be downgraded to a standard account.	[fair]	[fair]
you can always upgrade your account again.	[fair]	[fair]
skinvision collaborates with specific health insurers and corporates.	[fair]	[fair]
when you are admitted to the plan via one of these parties, terms of usage and duration of use may differ and are dependent on the contract and agreement skinvision has with the relevant party.	[fair]	[fair]
other services	[fair]	[fair]
in addition to the above services, skinvision provides other services that allows you to take photos with your standard camera, create folders to store, organize and comment on your pictures in order to track your skin over time.	[fair]	[fair]
you can also complete the questionnaire to assess your skin profile, access our library containing information on skin lesions and get information about the uv index of your location.	[fair]	[fair]
personal services	[fair]	[fair]
the skinvision service is exclusively provided to you, the user and individual account holder (personal services).	[fair]	[fair]
a user should not use their personal account to perform smart checks or skin checks on other individuals’ skin spots.	[fair]	[fair]

SPECIALNUMBER	[fair]	[fair]
when not to use the assessment	[fair]	[fair]
skinvision recommends you to carefully read the instructions for use before you use the smart check.	[fair]	[fair]
do not use the app to take a photo of a skin spot that:	[fair]	[fair]
is close in color with the surrounding skin, for example a skin spot on (very) dark skin (type v and vi per the fitzpatrick scale) or on sunburnt skin,	[fair]	[fair]
has been previously damaged,	[fair]	[fair]
is surrounded by non-intact skin (e.g. open sores, ulcers, bleeding, scabs),	[fair]	[fair]
is under the nail(s),	[fair]	[fair]
is close to a (visible) area with scar(s),	[fair]	[fair]
contains foreign matter (e.g. marker, tattoo, sunscreen, skin cream, powder, etc.),	[fair]	[fair]
is covered by a significant amount of hair,	[fair]	[fair]
is on mucosal surfaces (e.g. lips, genitals),	[fair]	[fair]
is on or near a skinfold or,	[fair]	[fair]
is not on human skin.	[fair]	[fair]
the lesion should be photographed directly (e.g. without the use of a dermascope, lens, mirror, or other appliances).	[fair]	[fair]
when the smart check is made for a lesion that is subject to any of the aforementioned contraindications, the smart check may be influenced in an unpredictable way.	[fair]	[fair]
in case you have doubts about a skin lesion or when it changes, is irritated or bleeds, always consult with your doctor.	[fair]	[fair]
the contra indications only apply for the smart check.	[fair]	[fair]
the contra indications are not applicable when you want to take pictures to monitor your skin lesions for changes without submitting for a smart check, or when you want to share your photo with your doctor.	[fair]	[fair]

prohibition of class and representative actions and non-individualized relief	[fair]	[fair]
you and ebay agree that each of us may bring claims against the other only on an individual basis and not as a plaintiff or class member in any purported class, or representative or private attorney general action or proceeding.	[fair]	[fair]

all other claims will be arbitrated.	[fair]	[fair]

arbitration procedures	[fair]	[fair]
arbitration is more informal than a lawsuit in court.	[fair]	[fair]
arbitration uses a neutral arbitrator instead of a judge or jury, and court review of an arbitration award is very limited.	[fair]	[fair]
however, an arbitrator can award the same damages and relief on an individual basis that a court can award to an individual.	[fair]	[fair]
an arbitrator should apply the terms of the user agreement as a court would.	[fair]	[fair]
all issues are for the arbitrator to decide, except that issues relating to arbitrability, the scope or enforceability of this agreement to arbitrate, or the interpretation of section SPECIALNUMBER of this agreement to arbitrate ("prohibition of class and representative actions and non-individualized relief"), shall be for a court of competent jurisdiction to decide.	[fair]	[fair]

SPECIALNUMBER	[fair]	[fair]
cancellation policy	[fair]	[fair]
SPECIALNUMBER	[fair]	[fair]
terms of repayment in the case of cancellation	[fair]	[fair]
only trips with booking are the subject of this cancellation policy; blablacar does not offer any guarantee, of any nature, in the case of cancellation for any reason, by a passenger or a driver, of a trip without booking.	[fair]	[fair]
the cancellation of a seat on a trip with booking by the driver or the passenger after booking confirmation is subject to the stipulations hereunder:	[fair]	[fair]
–    in the case of cancellation owing to the driver, the passenger is refunded the whole of the sum paid (i.e. the cost contribution and the related service fees).	[fair]	[fair]
this is notably the case when driver cancels a trip or has not arrived at the meeting point SPECIALNUMBER minutes after the agreed time;	[fair]	[fair]
–    in the case of cancellation owing to the passenger:	[fair]	[fair]
if the passenger cancels more than SPECIALNUMBER hours before the planned departure time as mentioned in the advert, the passenger shall only be refunded the cost contribution.	[fair]	[fair]
the service fees are retained by blablacar and the driver does not receive any sum of any nature;	[fair]	[fair]
if the passenger cancels SPECIALNUMBER hours or less before the planned departure time as mentioned in the advert, and more than thirty minutes after the booking confirmation, the passenger shall be refunded half the cost contribution paid at the time of booking, the service fees are retained by blablacar, and the driver receives SPECIALNUMBER% of the cost contribution;	[fair]	[fair]
if the passenger cancels SPECIALNUMBER hours or less before the planned departure time, as mentioned in the advert, and thirty minutes or less after the booking confirmation, the passenger is refunded the whole of the cost contribution.	[fair]	[fair]
the service fees are retained by blablacar and the driver does not receive any sum of any nature;	[fair]	[fair]
if the passenger cancels after the planned departure time as mentioned in the advert, or if he has not arrived at the meeting place SPECIALNUMBER minutes after the agreed time, no refund shall be issued.	[fair]	[fair]
the driver shall be compensated with the whole of the cost contribution and the service fees are kept by blablacar.	[fair]	[fair]
when cancellation occurs before departure and owing to the passenger, the seat(s) cancelled by the passenger shall be automatically made available to other passengers, who may book them online and they are accordingly subject to the conditions of these t&cs.	[fair]	[fair]
blablacar appreciates, in its sole discretion, on the basis of the available information, the legitimacy of the reimbursement requests.	[fair]	[fair]

(v)    to make the trip as described in the advert (notably with regard to using or not using the motorway) and to respect the times and places agreed with the other members (notably meeting place and drop-off point);	[fair]	[fair]

(vii)    to use a vehicle in good working order and which complies with the applicable legal provisions and customs, notably with an up-to-date mot certificate;	[fair]	[fair]
(viii)    to communicate to blablacar or any passenger who so requests your driving licence, your car registration certificate, your insurance certificate, your mot certificate and any document demonstrating your capacity to use the vehicle as driver on the platform;	[fair]	[fair]
(ix)    in the case of holdup or change to the time or the trip, to inform your passengers without delay;	[fair]	[fair]
(x)    in the case of a cross-border trip, to hold and keep available to the passenger and any authority that may so request any document evidencing your identity and your right to cross the border;	[fair]	[fair]
(xi)    to wait for passengers at the agreed meeting place for at least SPECIALNUMBER minutes after the agreed time;	[fair]	[fair]
(xii)    not to post an advert relative to a vehicle you do not own or that you are not authorised to use for the purposes of carpooling;	[fair]	[fair]
(xiii)    to ensure you can be contacted by your passengers by phone on the number registered on your profile;	[fair]	[fair]
(xiv)    not to generate any profit via the platform;	[fair]	[fair]
(xv)    not to have any contraindication or medical incapacity for driving;	[fair]	[fair]
(xvi)    to behave appropriately and responsibly during the trip, and in compliance with the spirit of carpooling;	[fair]	[fair]
(xvii)    not to decline any booking based on race, color, ethnicity, national origin, religion, sexual orientation, marital status, disability, physical appearance, marital status, pregnancy, special vulnerability due to their economic situation, name, place of residence, health, political opinion, age.	[fair]	[fair]

“fork”	[fair]	[fair]
